    "text": "# Atlas query playbook — 3.3.16\r\n\r\nRetained reads copy the `analyze_result` object and the SQL in\r\n[result-reader.md](result-reader.md).\r\nEQL stays `{:entity ... :query ...}` inside `execute_eql.query`.\r\n\r\n## Choose the fact capability\r\n\r\nStandard: monitor/history → pinned report-query/rows. Interactive: explore-query/rows.\r\nThe retained 3.2 long-text endpoints are compatibility-only. Read the active\r\nsection, not all examples. Use live list_entities/list_attributes/tool schemas and\r\nreuse discovery within the same environment. Dates/Vendors below are examples,\r\nnot run defaults. EQL wrapper is `{:entity ... :query ...}`, never Datalog.\r\n\r\nThe examples use plain joins accepted by the observed Studio. They are NOT a\r\nverified paging API. Report3.3 source default/max is1000; compact Top10 bound is221. Other\r\ncollections retain default200/max1000. Older report deployments may default200\r\nand cut off a221-row report unless supported source paging/limit is supplied.\r\nMCP source paging/sorting must use its actually supported schema. Do not paste\r\nparameterized joins into Studio after semantic.studio/query-shape rejection.\r\nIncomplete source results cannot be repaired by paging their truncated handle.\r\n\r\nComplete plain-join projections for monitor/report/history are in\r\n[structured-queries.eql](structured-queries.eql); below are readable examples.\r\nRead the complete example for HISTORICAL_FULL/explicit-target TEST: the short\r\nevent-list projection below alone cannot support Country count/ratio cross-checks.\r\n\r\n## Monitor (standard route)\r\n```clojure\r\n{:entity {:pastdue.monitor-query/calendar-mode \"WEEKDAYS\"\r\n          :pastdue.monitor-query/relative-change-threshold 0.10}\r\n :query [{:pastdue.monitor-query/countries\r\n           [:pastdue.monitor-fact/country-code\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/target-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/previous-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/calendar-mode\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/target-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/previous-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/target-open-count\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/previous-open-count\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/target-past-due-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/previous-past-due-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/relative-ratio-change\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/percentage-point-change\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/business-status\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/data-status\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/expected-target-date\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/target-lag-workdays\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/missing-workdays-between\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/has-comparable-snapshots\r\n            :pastdue.monitor-fact/snapshot-gap-days]}\r\n ]}\r\n```\r\nConsume complete Country facts before locally selecting authorized US/CA. Use\r\ntrusted runtime America/Los_Angeles today, not latest snapshot as a clock.\r\nNormal daily only drills TRIGGERED/ZERO_BASELINE_INCREASE with valid comparable\r\npositive Open and data OK/DELAYED. Test forces applicable valid pairs, warns stale.\r\nAn explicitly requested test/report target uses history-query/comparisons with\r\nrange-start-date=range-end-date=target and the same full Country projection.\r\nThis returns its actual prior eligible snapshot even if the target didn't trigger.\r\nDo not use trigger-events to find nontrigger test pairs or silently substitute\r\nthe latest monitor dates. An arbitrary explicit P/T uses explore PAIR/EXPLICIT.\r\n\r\n## Report (standard route, seven inputs)\r\n```clojure\r\n{:entity {:pastdue.report-query/country-code \"CA\"\r\n          :pastdue.report-query/target-snapshot-date \"2026-10-26\"\r\n          :pastdue.report-query/previous-snapshot-date \"2026-10-23\"\r\n          :pastdue.report-query/calendar-mode \"WEEKDAYS\"\r\n          :pastdue.report-query/selection-mode \"POSITIVE_TOP_N\"\r\n          :pastdue.report-query/top-n 10\r\n          :pastdue.report-query/vendor-code 0}\r\n :query [{:pastdue.report-query/rows\r\n           [:pastdue.report-fact/scope-key\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/record-type\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/country-code\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/vendor-code\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/vendor-name\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/calendar-mode\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/selection-mode\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/metric-version\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/business-timezone\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/queried-at-local\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/scope-status\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/dimension\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/eta-value\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/row-kind\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/contribution-rank\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/selected-vendor-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/past-due-count-change\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-open-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-open-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-country-open\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-country-open\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-country-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-country-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-country-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-country-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-vendor-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-vendor-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-country-contribution\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-country-contribution\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/delta-country-contribution\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/positive-contribution-share\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/selected-positive-coverage\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/display-rank\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/expected-bucket-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/other-bucket-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/increase-total\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/decrease-total\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/net-change\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-total\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-total\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/previous-expected-total\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/target-expected-total\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/counts-match\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/required\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/past-due-count-relative-change\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/expected-record-count\r\n            :pastdue.report-fact/row-ordinal]}\r\n ]}\r\n```\r\nRead one COUNTRY_CONTROL before consuming metrics; it need not be physically\r\nfirst. Then group VENDOR_METRICS and summaries by Country+Vendor+pair, and\r\nVENDOR_CHECK by dimension=code/source/us. BUCKET rank≤5 and OTHER rank6 are\r\nserver summaries. expected-record-count includes the control; raw bucket\r\ncoverage = BUCKET rows + Other's other-bucket-count, not summary row count.\r\nRequired counts-match/total/expected-total checks are mandatory even when empty.\r\nReconcile COUNTRY_CONTROL target/previous-country-past-due-count, -open and\r\n-ratio to the pinned monitor/history facts before combining captures. Drift is\r\nexplicit PARTIAL/UNVERIFIED unless paired raw evidence proves a hard conflict;\r\nqueried-at-local is capture evidence, never a cross-query transaction guarantee.\r\nUS summary is already joint Code=US×Source for EVERY Country. Do not call separate legacy dimension\r\ncollections. SINGLE_VENDOR changes selection-mode, actual vendor-code and top-n=1;\r\nit has no positive-only filter. Pin nearest-eligible P from monitor/history.\r\n\r\n## Flexible analysis: all 20 inputs, explicit denominator and complete projection\r\nThis example asks for all current Vendor×Code shares within each Vendor.\r\n```clojure\r\n{:entity {:pastdue.explore-query/country-code \"CA\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/time-basis \"CURRENT\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/target-snapshot-date \"1970-01-01\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/previous-snapshot-date \"1970-01-01\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/range-start-date \"1970-01-01\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/range-end-date \"1970-01-01\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/calendar-mode \"WEEKDAYS\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/pair-policy \"PREVIOUS_AVAILABLE\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/grain \"VENDOR_CODE\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/vendor-code 0\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/eta-code-filter \"\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/source-filter \"\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/department-type-filter \"\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/master-primary-filter \"\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/master-manager-filter \"\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/denominator-scope \"WITHIN_VENDOR\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/ranking-metric \"NONE\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/sort-direction \"ASC\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/change-selection \"ALL\"\r\n          :pastdue.explore-query/top-n 0}\r\n :query [{:pastdue.explore-query/rows\r\n           [:pastdue.explore-fact/scope-key\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/record-type\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/scope-status\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/country-code\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/vendor-code\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/vendor-name\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/target-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/previous-snapshot-date\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/calendar-mode\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/time-basis\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/grain\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/eta-code\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/eta-source\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/denominator-scope\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/metric-version\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/organization-basis\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/business-timezone\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/queried-at-local\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/target-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/previous-past-due-count\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/target-open-count\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/previous-open-count\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/target-ratio-denominator\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/previous-ratio-denominator\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/target-share-denominator\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/previous-share-denominator\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/target-country-open\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/previous-country-open\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/past-due-count-change\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/target-past-due-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/previous-past-due-ratio\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/target-past-due-share\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/previous-past-due-share\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/past-due-count-relative-change\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/past-due-ratio-change\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/delta-country-contribution\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/selection-rank\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/expected-record-count\r\n            :pastdue.explore-fact/row-ordinal]}\r\n ]}\r\n```\r\nOne CONTROL plus FACT rows, with expected-record-count including CONTROL.\r\nNon-PAIR previous date=1970-01-01 and previous counts are placeholders, not a\r\nzero baseline to diagnose. Sparse control default values are not observations.\r\nUse returned business-timezone/queried-at-local and actual DST label/offset from\r\ntrusted runtime; an ambiguous fall-back local timestamp without its instant/offset\r\nmust not be resolved by guessing.     "text": "# Evidence and completion — 3.3.16\r\n\r\nThis is an Agent procedure, not an installed runtime gate. Query execution,\r\nretrieval and delivery are separate; none proves independent final-output accuracy.\r\n\r\n## A. Source and identity\r\n\r\nRetain actual input, execute response, handle, exposed environment/version and\r\ncapture metadata. A handle must originate in that request. Missing linkage is\r\nUNVERIFIED; a proven wrong attachment is ASSOCIATION_ERROR. Do not repair it by\r\nmatching plausible numbers. Start with serial Country requests.\r\n\r\nRequire scope-status=OK before interpreting selection. Compare COUNTRY_CONTROL\r\ndedicated previous/target-country-past-due-count, -country-open and -country-ratio\r\nto the pinned monitor/history. See result-reader.md's exact binding table; Vendor\r\ncount/Open fields on this control are placeholders and MUST NOT be compared as\r\nCountry measurements. Missing projection means not read, not zero. No zero-value\r\nexemption applies to the dedicated fields. Dates/keys do not prove cross-query\r\ntransactions. Different captures that disagree remain unverified pending evidence;\r\nnever silently overwrite one with another. Counts exact; ratios use metric tolerance.\r\nA genuine cross-capture mismatch limits attribution to that pinned monitor event.\r\nIndependently verified report-scope facts may be described as such, with their own\r\ndates/denominators and explicit limitation; never silently merge captures. Unknown\r\nhandle provenance limits ALL facts dependent on that handle, even if totals match.\r\n\r\n## B. Retrieval\r\n\r\nUse the playbook's two-layer reading procedure. Track separately:\r\n- source expected records and actual unique rows from all EQL pages;\r\n- retained-result rows actually read, across all reading partitions/pages;\r\n- cell truncation affecting each field, not only top-level truncated?.\r\n\r\nA statement is complete when `truncation.rows?` is false, `cells?` is false, and\r\n`rows-shown` equals `rows-total`. `row-count` is the retained table. One\r\nstatement's `rows-total` may be lower than `expected_record_count` because the\r\nother statement holds the remaining record types. Add the disjoint `rows-total`\r\nvalues. Equality with `expected_record_count` means the compact report was read.\r\nRender Code, Source, signed totals and diagnosis from those rows. Exactly\r\nexpected rows with cut-off cells is not complete retrieval. A successful\r\nSELECT over an incomplete retained result is not source completeness. A truncated\r\nname limits name display, not independently intact numeric facts under verified\r\nCountry/Vendor identity. A truncated identity/scope field requires a supported\r\nfull-value read or leaves the affected association unverified. Don't silently\r\nstrip an ellipsis or assume it was part of the original value.\r\n\r\n## C. Server reconciliation and compact representation\r\n\r\n- Exactly one COUNTRY_CONTROL, including valid empty positive selection.\r\n- Exactly selected-vendor-count unique VENDOR_METRICS, with stable returned ranks.\r\n- Exactly one VENDOR_CHECK per selected Vendor×code/source/us, including empty paths.\r\n- Code and Source each match Vendor two-period PD. Joint us matches Code=US PD.\r\n- required=false with zero expected joint totals is NOT_APPLICABLE, not failure.\r\n  Nonzero US expected/visible counts with required=false is inconsistent.\r\n- Required checks need counts-match=true and matching expected/actual totals.\r\n- Each summary BUCKET has unique identity/display-rank; OTHER is one server\r\n  remainder, not UNKNOWN. BUCKET rows plus Other represented bucket counts must\r\n  match expected-bucket-count. Empty nonzero-total dimensions are not valid empties.\r\n- Never add Code and Source together. Read signed totals from VENDOR_CHECK;\r\n  Other net delta does not expose its internal increase/decrease composition.\r\n\r\nThese support server full-population reconciliation and compact-summary coverage,\r\nNOT client independent raw-bucket audit. Generic source-completeness UNKNOWN alone\r\nneither proves failure nor overrides valid identities/counts/checks. Record the\r\nactual evidence and any remaining source limitation.\r\n\r\n## D. Delivery\r\n\r\nBefore sending, compare selected Vendor set/order to ranking rows; verify each\r\nhas metrics, main Code cell, main Source cell, check totals and status or local\r\nfailure. Keep a delivery ledger separate from retrieval: per Country, actual\r\nselected Vendor codes/ranks → metrics → main Code (≤5 by |net| from returned\r\nrows) → main Source (≤5 by |net|) → both signed totals → analysis status or\r\nprecise local failure. Count a ranking row as delivered only when all these\r\napplicable cells are actually present, not merely planned or available in a handle.\r\nMain cells use returned bucket identities and both-period counts in P→T form;\r\ndo not invent buckets. Full six-column tables are on-request only and are not\r\nrequired for standard delivery. Check language, dates, signs and delta-vs-level\r\nfield binding against original rows. This self-review is not independent\r\nvalidation and cannot guarantee final accuracy.\r\n\r\nReport the actual analysis scope as complete/partial/unavailable/not required.\r\nKeep actual business trigger and freshness separate. At the end report selected\r\nVendor count, fully delivered ranking-row count, delivered-with-local-failure\r\ncount and not-yet-delivered codes/ranks. A local failure placeholder is not valid\r\nETA analysis; its presence makes the report honest, not the failed data complete.\r\nA source/read success with missing Vendor ranking rows or empty ETA cells is an\r\nincomplete delivery. Confirmed wrong rendered facts are REPORT_ERROR; correct the\r\noutput, not the valid source. No model-issued overall PASS, invented evaluation\r\nscore or source-hash proof of final text correctness. If independent validation\r\nis asked about, say 'not performed' unless real evidence exists; do not burden\r\nevery successful Vendor with that technical disclaimer.\r\n\r\nTEST shows forced vs real trigger, Countries/Vendors exercised, required steps\r\nexecuted and missing/unexercised scope. Zero-positive selection can complete its\r\napplicable empty path but did not exercise multi-Vendor ETA. No sending/escalation.\r\n\r\n## Local failures and retry\r\n\r\nA failed Code query/check does not erase Source or metrics. Failed joint evidence\r\nlimits its diagnostic, not ordinary Source display. Never substitute zero for\r\nquery failure or old positive-only drivers for signed changes.\r\n\r\nDATA_CONFLICT requires TWO contradictory original responses under the same full\r\nsemantic identity and common field. Model drafts are not evidence. Projection,\r\nformat, time/version/scope differences alone are not same-fact contradictions.\r\nSingle-response mismatch is reconciliation failure, not paired conflict.\r\n\r\nOne evidence-based correction retry per logical request. Unsupported result-read\r\nsyntax may be corrected after discovering actual support. Cancellation alone does\r\nnot identify its initiator; reducing projection/limit doesn't fix source aggregation.\r\nNo permissions bypass, per-Vendor retry storm or fabricated platform root cause.\r\nFor result-reader selector validation, follow result-reader.md: initial call plus\r\nONE corrected call that removes conflicting keys, never an identical third attempt.\r\nThe correction must also preserve the actual execute handle and connection;\r\nchanging analysis_version isn't a correction. Discovery success is not rows success.\r\nMixed-selector errors alone don't prove host incompatibility. Actual required or\r\ntransformed conflicting keys need separate evidence before blaming an adapter.\r\nDon't fix tool arguments by changing EQL/source SQL. The supplied manual discovery\r\ntest does not certify this Agent release or its report output.\r\n"

    "text": "# Report modules — 3.3.16 (single layout authority)\r\n\r\nUse structured facts, not 3.2 long Markdown blocks. Same modules and column order\r\nin Chinese and English; choose ONE language. Code/Vendor names remain literal data.\r\nInteractive answers compose only relevant modules. Standard reports use all\r\napplicable modules below; explicit brief/custom output discloses its reduced scope.\r\n\r\n## Shared formatting\r\n\r\nDate YYYY-MM-DD, pairs previous→target, integers without thousands separators,\r\npositive changes +, negative changes −, zero 0. Ratios/shares/relative changes:\r\n100×returned ratio, exactly two decimals %. Contribution levels/changes: 100×ratio,\r\nexactly four decimals pp. Round for display only; don't recompute formulas.\r\nIf a nonzero change rounds to zero, use '<0.01%' / '<0.0001 pp' with its actual\r\ndirection in words rather than a misleading signed zero. Exact zero remains 0.\r\nEscape table delimiters/newlines in names. Do not change raw identity to fit display.\r\nNull relative change with P=0,T>0: 零基线上升 / Zero-baseline increase; P=T=0:\r\n无变化 / No change. Missing, failed and unqueried are not zero or the same '—'.\r\nUse 无数据/No data only for verified successful empty results; 未查询/Not queried,\r\n查询失败/Query failed, 未验证完整/Completeness unverified describe their actual cases.\r\n\r\n## Standard section order\r\n\r\n1. **运行信息与结论 / Run information and conclusion**: DAILY/HISTORICAL/TEST,\r\n   resolved language, America/Los_Angeles, actual P/T per Country. Show actual\r\n   PST/PDT+offset only with trusted instant evidence. TEST title [TEST]/[测试],\r\n   preserve actual trigger and show TEST_FORCED/THRESHOLD_MET separately.\r\n2. **Country 指标 / Country metrics**: all requested Countries, default US→CA.\r\n3. For each applicable **{Country} — {P}→{T}**:\r\n   - **Vendor 排名与主要 ETA 变化 / Vendor ranking with main ETA changes**\r\n   - **Country Vendor 总结分析 / Country Vendor analysis summary**\r\n   - **覆盖率与限制 / Coverage and limitations**\r\n4. **跨 Country 总结 / Cross-Country summary** (multiple Countries only).\r\n5. **执行范围与缺失项 / Execution scope and gaps**: compact actual source checks,\r\n   sum of disjoint statement rows versus expected_record_count, Vendors delivered\r\n   vs selected, exact missing scope. A complete split read still renders every\r\n   selected Vendor's ranking row with main Code/Source cells and signed-total check.\r\n\r\nNo-trigger Countries still appear in metrics; ordinary drilldown is not required.\r\nValid zero-positive selection shows explicit empty selection, not fabricated rows.\r\nHistory events-only stops at event listing. No current monitor mislabeled historical.\r\n\r\n## Country metrics columns (exact order)\r\n\r\n| 中文 | English | Binding |\r\n|---|---|---|\r\n| Country | Country | country-code |\r\n| 业务状态 | Business status | business-status |\r\n| 数据状态 | Data status | data-status |\r\n| 上期日期 | Previous date | previous-snapshot-date |\r\n| 本期日期 | Target date | target-snapshot-date |\r\n| 间隔天数 | Gap days | snapshot-gap-days |\r\n| 逾期 上期→本期 | Past due previous→target | previous/target-past-due-count |\r\n| Open 上期→本期 | Open previous→target | previous/target-open-count |\r\n| 逾期率 上期→本期 | Ratio previous→target | previous/target-past-due-ratio |\r\n| 相对变化 | Relative change | relative-ratio-change |\r\n| 百分点变化 | Point change | percentage-point-change (ratio→pp) |\r\n\r\nDates not adjacent: say 较上一可用快照 / Compared with previous available snapshot,\r\nnot 'yesterday'; include calendar gap and delay/missing-weekday warnings when returned.\r\nCopy every column for the chosen language, in this order. Do not drop Previous\r\ndate, Target date or Gap days, and do not rename the headers.\r\n\r\n## Vendor ranking with main ETA changes\r\n\r\nMetric columns bind **VENDOR_METRICS only**. ETA cells bind returned ETA bucket\r\nrows and VENDOR_CHECK for that same Vendor. Never read Vendor totals from ETA\r\nbucket rows. Never add Code and Source together. Never invent OTHER constituents.\r\n\r\n| 中文 | English | Binding |\r\n|---|---|---|\r\n| 排名 | Rank | contribution-rank |\r\n| Vendor Code／名称 | Vendor code / name | vendor-code + vendor-name |\r\n| 逾期 上期→本期 | Past due previous→target | previous/target-past-due-count |\r\n| 净变化 | Net change | past-due-count-change |\r\n| Open 上期→本期 | Open previous→target | previous/target-open-count |\r\n| Vendor 逾期率 上期→本期 | Vendor ratio previous→target | previous/target-vendor-ratio |\r\n| Country 贡献变化 pp | Country contribution change pp | delta-country-contribution |\r\n| 正向贡献占比 | Positive contribution share | positive-contribution-share |\r\n| 主要 Code 变化 | Main Code changes | returned Code ETA buckets (see cell rules) |\r\n| 主要 Source 变化 | Main Source changes | returned Source ETA buckets (see cell rules) |\r\n| 核对 | Check totals | VENDOR_CHECK signed totals |\r\n| 分析状态 | Analysis status | actual scoped checks/retrieval, not invented raw field |\r\n\r\nBind overview and ETA cells to the SAME Vendor identity and rank. SINGLE_VENDOR\r\nretains actual rank and can have nonpositive delta; positive-share is not\r\napplicable there if its contribution is nonpositive. Copy every ranking column\r\nfor the chosen language, in this order. Contribution changes use exactly four\r\ndecimal places.\r\n\r\n### Main Code / Main Source cell rules\r\n\r\nFor **each dimension independently** (Code separate from Source):\r\n\r\n1. Start from that Vendor's **returned** compact ETA rows for the dimension\r\n   (Top5 BUCKET rows + returned OTHER if present). Do not invent OTHER.\r\n2. Rank those returned rows by absolute net-change `|净变化|` descending.\r\n3. Take at most **5** rows. If fewer than 5 returned rows exist, show only the\r\n   actual returned rows. If zero returned rows after a successful empty read,\r\n   write 无 / None. If the dimension failed or was unread, write the concrete\r\n   failure/unread status — never fabricate values.\r\n4. Format each kept row as `{ETA value} {P}→{T} ({signed net})`. Join with\r\n   `；` (Chinese) or `; ` (English). Preserve literal ETA identity spelling.\r\n5. OTHER display name remains 其他（N项）/Other (N items) when that row is kept.\r\n   Do not re-rank by display-rank for this cell; absolute net-change is the sort.\r\n\r\nSigned net uses the shared formatting rules (+/−/0). Relative change and\r\nCountry contribution pp are **not** required inside these cells.\r\n\r\n### Check totals cell\r\n\r\nFrom VENDOR_CHECK for that Vendor, one compact line:\r\n`Code +I/−D/净N；Source +I/−D/净N` (or English equivalents).\r\nI = increase-total, D = decrease-total (negative signed as returned), N = net-change.\r\nIf a dimension's check failed/unread, state that side's status instead of numbers.\r\n\r\n## Country Vendor analysis summary (immediately after ranking)\r\n\r\nWrite concise grounded interpretation: leading selected contributors; material\r\nincreases and improvement offsets; verified conditional Code=US Source meaning;\r\npriority checks; scope limits. Use rule IDs/meanings from eta-diagnostic-rules.edn.\r\nPreserve Vendor identity. Don't invent Country-wide aggregates from Top10 or add\r\nCode and Source. No standalone US joint-source column/table, no generic tutorial\r\ninstead of actual observed evidence. If no applicable joint activity, omit that\r\ninterpretation; if needed evidence failed, disclose only the affected limitation.\r\nUnknown/Other Source has no guessed classification. Compact Top5+Other and the\r\nmain-change cells support only their visible buckets and returned signed totals,\r\nnot unseen constituents.\r\n\r\n## Full six-column ETA tables (on request only)\r\n\r\nStandard DAILY / HISTORICAL / TEST reports **do not** instantiate per-Vendor\r\n`### #{rank} …` sections or six-column Code/Source tables. Those tables are an\r\ninteractive / follow-up module when the user asks for one Vendor's full ETA\r\nbreakdown (or explicitly requests full tables).\r\n\r\nWhen requested, use exactly these columns in this order:\r\n\r\n| ETA 值 / ETA value | 上期数量 / Previous count | 本期数量 / Target count | 净变化 / Net change | 相对变化 / Relative change | Country 贡献变化 pp / Country contribution change pp |\r\n|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|\r\n\r\nChoose localized headers, not bilingual duplication. BUCKET order=display-rank;\r\nOTHER is 其他（N项）/Other (N items), server-computed counts and relative change.\r\nDo not invent Other when no OTHER record exists. If the server returned OTHER\r\nwith zero counts but positive other-bucket-count, display it. Do not re-rank\r\nsummary rows. A failed/unread dimension retains its heading and concrete\r\nfailure/empty status, never a fabricated table. Include metrics line and\r\nCode/Source signed totals with diagnosis when expanding a Vendor on request.\r\n\r\n### Optional on-request Vendor skeleton\r\n\r\n```text\r\n### #{rank} {vendor_code} — {vendor_name}\r\n指标变化 / Metric changes: PD P→T; Open P→T; Vendor ratio P→T;\r\nCountry contribution P→T; contribution change pp.\r\n\r\nETA Code 变化 / ETA Code changes\r\n[the six-column table: ALL returned Top5 BUCKET rows + returned OTHER]\r\n\r\nETA Source 变化 / ETA Source changes\r\n[the same six-column table, independent Source facts]\r\n\r\n变化摘要与诊断 / Change summary and diagnosis\r\nCode: increases +I; decreases −D; net N.\r\nSource: increases +I; decreases −D; net N.\r\n[Evidence-based worsening, improvement offsets, remaining risk, suggested check.]\r\n```\r\n\r\n## Coverage, context and length\r\n\r\nCoverage = selected-positive-coverage once, not sum of repeated metadata:\r\nTop10覆盖全部正向贡献的X% / Top10 covers X% of all positive contributions.\r\nNever describe it as the explained fraction of net Country increase. Mention\r\nnegative offsets and limit conclusions to selected Vendors.\r\n\r\nOwner/contacts/PO remain on request, CURRENT and display-only; standard report\r\nneedn't repeat 'not queried' under every Vendor. If requested, show actual result\r\nor status under that Vendor. PO quotas remain unchanged.\r\n\r\nKeep every selected Vendor as one ranking row with main Code, main Source and\r\ncheck cells in the same answer. Save space by removing repeated definitions,\r\nstatus matrices and raw JSON, not by dropping selected Vendor rows or ETA cells.\r\nA predicted response budget is not a reason to stop after metric-only ranking.\r\nDo not promise unsupported automatic continuation. [
  {
    "type": "text",
    "text": "---\nname: pastdue-html-report\ndescription: After a successful PastDue DAILY or TEST standard Markdown report, build a verified JSON payload and emit one Ops/Demo HTML file without re-querying Atlas.\n---\n\n# PastDue HTML Report — companion\n\nHTML is a **presentation companion** only. Markdown from `pastdue-analysis` stays\nauthoritative. This skill does not render or reinterpret analysis modules, does not\nre-query Atlas, and does not scrape delivered Markdown as the source of truth.\n\n## 1. When to load\n\nLoad and follow this skill **only after** `pastdue-analysis` has completed a\n**successful standard DAILY or TEST** Markdown report in the same turn:\n\n- All applicable `report-format.md` modules delivered in the resolved language.\n- Every selected Vendor has a ranking row with main Code/Source/check cells or an\n  explicit local failure — not metric-only ranking.\n- Execution scope and gaps stated; no self-awarded overall PASS.\n\nDo **not** auto-emit HTML for HISTORICAL events-only lists, INTERACTIVE partial\nanswers, incomplete gates, or failed standard reports.\n\n## 2. Build the payload\n\nConstruct a JSON object from **in-memory verified bindings** already used for\nMarkdown — same facts, same ordinals, same failure disclosures.\n\nRead [payload contract](references/payload-contract.md) for shape, required keys,\ncolumn order, and formatting rules. Layout and number-copy authority remain in\n`skill/pastdue-analysis/references/report-format.md`; do not recompute metrics\nor fork formulas here.\n\nSet `meta.mode` to `DAILY` or `TEST` only. Embed nothing that was not verified\nduring analysis.\n\n## 3. Gate\n\nBefore writing any HTML, apply the gate checklist in\n[payload-contract.md](references/payload-contract.md) (hand-check is enough;\nrunning `scripts/payload_gate.py` / `render_ops_shell.py` is optional).\n\nHard rejects (do **not** write HTML):\n\n- `meta.mode` not `DAILY`/`TEST`\n- empty `per_country[].ranking`\n- any `selected-vendor-codes` entry missing a ranking row\n- missing/empty `summary` or `coverage`, or missing `scope_gaps`\n\nRanking rows **with** explicit local-failure status are allowed (same honesty as\nMarkdown). Do not park Vendor facts only in page JS while leaving `ranking: []`.\n\nOn gate failure:\n\n- **Skip HTML entirely** — do not write a partial or “complete-looking” file.\n- Disclose the gate reason in **one or two sentences** in chat only.\n- Do not modify Markdown already delivered.\n\n## 4. Emit HTML\n\nRead [html-shell](references/html-shell.md) for document structure, section IDs,\nmode toggle, naming, and reduced-motion rules.\n\n**Preferred agent path (no Atlas, no runtime Python):** emit equivalent HTML directly,\nmatching `html-shell.md` and the payload contract (same section IDs, `data-mode`,\nembedded `#pastdue-report` JSON, column order, headers). Apply the gate checklist in\n`payload-contract.md` by hand before writing the file.\n\n**Optional local verification helper:** `scripts/render_ops_shell.py` is a\nfixture/CI aid for checking that the shell and gate behave as documented. It is not a\nrequired runtime service, not a Markdown report renderer, and not something the\nagent must invoke to deliver HTML:\n\n```bash\npython skill/pastdue-html-report/scripts/render_ops_shell.py <payload.json> -o <out.html>\n```\n\n`render_ops_shell(payload) -> str` is importable; it gates first and raises\n`ValueError(reason)` on failure (CLI: `gate failed: …`, exit 1, no file).\n\nThe `pastdue-analysis` ban on runtime Python/service still applies to the analysis\nitself. The helper is out-of-band verification tooling only.\n\nEnhance **Demo** motion in-page after the Ops baseline exists (`data-mode=demo`,\n`?mode=demo`, master timeline per html-shell). Ops remains fully readable without\nDemo JS.\n\n## 5. Write and disclose\n\nWrite exactly **one** self-contained `.html` file in the host-allowed working\ndirectory for this turn (do not read or write arbitrary paths outside that\nmounted workspace). Suggested name:\n`pastdue-{DAILY|TEST}-{countries}-{target-date}.html` (see html-shell).\n\n**Deliverable path vs skill-package hygiene**\n\n- Session deliverables go under the host working directory (e.g. `/sandbox/…`).\n- This skill’s `scripts/out/*.html` gitignore is **only** for local/CI samples and\n  skill-upload packaging. It does **not** mean delete or withhold session HTML.\n\n**Retention after write (narrow — not a blanket ban on all deletes)**\n\n- After a deliverable HTML is written and its path disclosed, **do not** clean it\n  up with `rm`/`delete` (or equivalent) and leave the user with no file.\n- Fixes: **overwrite** the same path with a corrected file, or skip writing until\n  the gate passes. Do not `rm` without a successful replacement write in the same\n  turn.\n- If the gate fails, simply do not write; no delete step is required.\n\nDisclose the written path in chat. On success, a brief note that HTML is a\ncompanion to the Markdown report is enough. On skip (gate failure), reason only —\nno file path.\n\n## 6. Animation skills (optional, same workspace)\n\nIf sibling animation skills are installed in the same agent workspace, use their\npatterns for Demo polish only. Prefer resolving by skill **name** when the host\nexposes installed skills (`diagram-animation`, `svg-animation`,\n`isometric-animation`). Do not require paths outside this skill package for\nHTML emit to succeed — Ops/Demo HTML works without them.\n\n| Skill name | Use |\n|------------|-----|\n| `diagram-animation` | Demo section beats; light Ops count-up / bar emphasis |\n| `svg-animation` | Status marks, light stroke accents |\n| `isometric-animation` | Demo decoration **only** when explicitly wanted — **Ops default off** |\n\nInvoke motion patterns only inside the emitted HTML; never change payload facts.\nDemo must not omit ranking rows, ETA/check cells, coverage, or `scope_gaps` for\npolish.\n\nRespect `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` — final composed state, no looping.\n\n## 7. Hard boundaries\n\n- **Never** modify Markdown already delivered in the analysis turn.\n- **Never** award or restate an overall PASS for the run.\n- **Never** re-query Atlas, re-execute EQL, or bind new facts inside this skill.\n- **Never** write HTML with empty `ranking` or Vendor tables only in ad-hoc page JS.\n- **Never** delete a disclosed session HTML deliverable without overwriting it in\n  the same turn.\n- HTML reformats verified bindings for display; it is not a second analysis pass.\n"
  }
]

[
  {
    "type": "text",
    "text": "# HTML report payload contract\n\nAgents embed the canonical report bindings as JSON in\n\n`<script type=\"application/json\" id=\"pastdue-report\">…</script>`.\n\nThe HTML skill **reformats for display only**. It does not re-query Atlas, re-run\nanalysis, or recompute metrics. Field meanings, section order, column order, and\nnumber copy rules are defined in\n`skill/pastdue-analysis/references/report-format.md` — follow that document;\ndo not fork formulas here.\n\nReference sample: `sample-payload.json` in this directory.\n\nImplementation gate: `skill/pastdue-html-report/scripts/payload_gate.py`\n(`gate_payload`, `REQUIRED_META_KEYS`, `COUNTRY_COLUMN_KEYS`, `VENDOR_RANKING_KEYS`).\n\n---\n\n## Top-level object\n\n| Key | Required | Role |\n|-----|----------|------|\n| `meta` | yes | Run metadata (`REQUIRED_META_KEYS`) |\n| `run` | yes | Conclusion, trigger flags, P/T pairs per Country |\n| `countries` | yes | Non-empty array; one row per Country metrics table row |\n| `per_country` | yes | Non-empty array; drilldown blocks (ranking + summary + coverage) |\n| `cross_country_summary` | no | Text for module 4 when multiple Countries apply |\n| `scope_gaps` | yes | Execution scope and gaps (may be an explicit empty structure) |\n\n---\n\n## `meta`\n\nAll keys in `REQUIRED_META_KEYS` (same tuple as `payload_gate.py`):\n\n```text\nmode\nlanguage\ntimezone\ncalendar-mode\ngenerated-at\nmetric-version\n```\n\n`mode` must be `DAILY` or `TEST` for HTML auto-delivery (see Gate below).\n`language` is the single resolved report language (`zh` or `en` per analysis delivery).\n\n---\n\n## `run`\n\n| Field | Type | Notes |\n|-------|------|-------|\n| `conclusion` | string | Run conclusion as already delivered in Markdown |\n| `trigger-flags` | string[] | e.g. `THRESHOLD_MET`, `TEST_FORCED` — preserve actual flags |\n| `pairs` | object | Map `country-code` → `{ previous-snapshot-date, target-snapshot-date }` |\n\nDate values in `pairs` use `YYYY-MM-DD`.\n\n---\n\n## `countries[]`\n\nEach element is one Country metrics row. **Key order for binding** (exact list from\n`COUNTRY_COLUMN_KEYS` in `payload_gate.py`):\n\n```text\ncountry-code\nbusiness-status\ndata-status\nprevious-snapshot-date\ntarget-snapshot-date\nsnapshot-gap-days\nprevious-past-due-count\ntarget-past-due-count\nprevious-open-count\ntarget-open-count\nprevious-past-due-ratio\ntarget-past-due-ratio\nrelative-ratio-change\npercentage-point-change\n```\n\nScalars in the payload are stored as returned by analysis (ratios as unit fractions,\ne.g. `0.2` not `20%`). HTML display applies `report-format.md` shared formatting.\n\nColumn header labels and table order: `report-format.md` § Country metrics columns.\n\n---\n\n## `per_country[]`\n\nOne block per Country that received Vendor drilldown in the standard report.\n\n| Field | Required | Notes |\n|-------|----------|-------|\n| `country-code` | yes | Matches a `countries[]` row |\n| `selected-vendor-codes` | yes | Every code must appear on a `ranking[]` row |\n| `ranking` | yes | Non-empty; one row per delivered selected Vendor |\n| `summary` | yes | Country Vendor analysis summary text as delivered |\n| `coverage` | yes | Coverage and limitations text as delivered |\n\n### `ranking[]` row keys\n\n**Exact order** (`VENDOR_RANKING_KEYS` in `payload_gate.py`):\n\n```text\ncontribution-rank\nvendor-code\nvendor-name\nprevious-past-due-count\ntarget-past-due-count\npast-due-count-change\nprevious-open-count\ntarget-open-count\nprevious-vendor-ratio\ntarget-vendor-ratio\ndelta-country-contribution\npositive-contribution-share\nmain-code-changes\nmain-source-changes\ncheck-totals\nanalysis-status\n```\n\nMetric columns bind **VENDOR_METRICS only**. The three ETA/check columns are\n**pre-rendered strings** copied from the Markdown delivery (same Vendor identity and\nrank as the overview row).\n\nGate requires **every** key in `VENDOR_RANKING_KEYS` on every ranking row, including\n`main-code-changes`, `main-source-changes`, `check-totals` and `analysis-status`.\n\nVendor ranking headers and order: `report-format.md` § Vendor ranking with main ETA changes.\n\n### HTML skill — ETA / Vendor hard rules\n\nFrom `report-format.md` (binding, not optional for HTML):\n\n- **Never invent OTHER** constituents or ETA buckets. Only display what the returned\n  compact rows / pre-built cell strings already contain.\n- **Never read Vendor totals from ETA bucket rows** and never recompute Vendor past-due\n  or open counts by summing buckets.\n- **Never add Code and Source together** into a single synthetic total.\n- Do not fabricate `analysis-status`; use actual scoped check/retrieval status strings.\n\nThe HTML layer must not expand `main-code-changes` / `main-source-changes` with buckets\nthat were not in the delivered analysis output.\n\n---\n\n## `cross_country_summary` (optional)\n\nWhen multiple Countries are in scope and the Markdown report included module 4,\ninclude a string (or structured text field) with the cross-Country summary as delivered.\nOmit the key when the module does not apply.\n\n---\n\n## `scope_gaps`\n\nRequired top-level key. Structure must support rendering module 5 (execution scope and\ngaps). An explicit “no gaps” payload is valid (e.g. `items: []` plus notes).\n\nExample shape (illustrative; follow actual delivery):\n\n```json\n{\n  \"items\": [],\n  \"notes\": \"无缺失项\"\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Display formatting (authority: `report-format.md`)\n\nApply **`skill/pastdue-analysis/references/report-format.md` § Shared formatting** when\nturning payload scalars and strings into HTML text. Summary for implementers:\n\n| Kind | Rule |\n|------|------|\n| Dates | `YYYY-MM-DD`; pairs `previous→target` |\n| Integers | No thousands separators |\n| Signed nets | Positive `+`, negative `−`, zero `0` |\n| Ratios / shares / relative changes | `100×` stored ratio, **two** decimal places `%` |\n| Contribution levels / `delta-country-contribution` | `100×` ratio, **four** decimal places `pp` |\n| Round for display only | Do not recompute underlying formulas in HTML |\n| Near-zero display | `<0.01%` / `<0.0001 pp` with direction in words when applicable |\n| Zero baseline | `report-format.md`: null relative with P=0,T>0 vs P=T=0 copy |\n| Main ETA cells / check totals | Use delivered strings; cell rules in `report-format.md` § Main Code / Main Source / Check totals |\n\nIf payload numbers and formatted Markdown disagree, the Markdown delivery is wrong —\nfix upstream bindings, not HTML math.\n\n---\n\n## Gate checklist (`gate_payload`)\n\nRun `gate_payload(payload)` before emitting HTML. Behavior matches\n`skill/pastdue-html-report/scripts/payload_gate.py` exactly:\n\n1. **`meta.mode`** ∈ `DAILY`, `TEST` — otherwise reject (e.g. `INTERACTIVE`, `HISTORICAL` events-only).\n2. **Every `REQUIRED_META_KEYS` entry** present on `meta`.\n3. **Top-level `scope_gaps`** present.\n4. **`countries`** present and non-empty; **every** row contains **all** `COUNTRY_COLUMN_KEYS`.\n5. **`per_country`** present and non-empty.\n6. For **each** `per_country` block:\n   - `country-code` appears in `countries[]`.\n   - `summary` and `coverage` present, non-null and non-empty strings.\n   - `ranking` non-empty (else reject with `{country-code}: empty ranking`).\n   - `selected-vendor-codes` is a **non-empty list** (DAILY/TEST standard reports always\n     select Vendors); for each code a ranking row with matching `vendor-code` exists.\n   - Every ranking row contains **all** `VENDOR_RANKING_KEYS`.\n\nOn success: `(True, \"ok\")`. On failure: `(False, reason)` — skip HTML and state the\nreason briefly (same honesty as analysis delivery gate).\n\nThe renderer CLI (`render_ops_shell.py`, optional verification helper) catches\n`ValueError` and `KeyError`, prints `gate failed: <reason>`, exits 1 and writes no file.\n\n---\n\n## Producer checklist\n\n1. Markdown standard modules delivered and gated per `report-format.md`.\n2. Build payload from the **same verified bindings** used for Markdown.\n3. `gate_payload` passes.\n4. Embed JSON in the HTML shell (`html-shell.md`, later tasks) without altering facts.\n"
  }
]

[
  {
    "type": "text",
    "text": "# HTML shell conventions (Ops baseline)\n\nThe Ops shell is a single self-contained HTML5 file produced deterministically from a\ngated payload (`payload-contract.md`). It reformats for display only; metrics and\nlayout authority stay in `skill/pastdue-analysis/references/report-format.md`.\n\n## Producing the file\n\nPreferred agent path: hand-author equivalent HTML matching this shell (no Atlas, no\nruntime Python). `scripts/render_ops_shell.py` is an **optional local verification\nhelper** for fixtures/CI, not a required runtime service or Markdown renderer.\n\nAgents may either:\n\n1. (Optional, verification only) Run the renderer:\n\n   ```bash\n   python skill/pastdue-html-report/scripts/render_ops_shell.py <payload.json> -o <out.html>\n   ```\n\n   `render_ops_shell(payload) -> str` is also importable. It calls `gate_payload` first\n   and raises `ValueError(reason)` on failure; the CLI prints `gate failed: <reason>`,\n   exits non-zero and writes no file. On gate failure agents skip HTML and state the\n   reason briefly.\n\n2. (Preferred) Hand-author equivalent HTML that matches this shell (same section IDs, `data-mode`\n   handling, embedded JSON script, column order and headers).\n\n## Document structure\n\n- `<html lang=\"zh|en\">` from `meta.language` (single language, no bilingual duplication).\n- Sticky `header.top`: report title (`[测试]`/`[TEST]` prefix when `meta.mode=TEST`),\n  run-mode badge (`TEST`/`DAILY`), view-mode badge (`OPS`/`DEMO`), `P/T` pairs from\n  `run.pairs`, and Ops/Demo toggle buttons (`data-set-mode`).\n- `<body data-mode=\"ops\">` is the default.\n- `<script type=\"application/json\" id=\"pastdue-report\">` embeds the full payload\n  (`json.dumps(..., ensure_ascii=False)`; `</` escaped as `<\\/` so JSON stays valid).\n\n## Section IDs (in order)\n\n| ID | Content |\n|----|---------|\n| `run` | Run information and conclusion, trigger flags, P/T |\n| `country-metrics` | Country metrics table (all `countries[]` rows) |\n| `country-{CODE}` | One per `per_country[]`: ranking table, summary, coverage (e.g. `country-US`) |\n| `cross-country` | Only when `cross_country_summary` is present |\n| `scope-gaps` | Execution scope and gaps (`scope_gaps`) |\n\n## Tables\n\nHeader text and column order follow `report-format.md` (Chinese for `zh`, English for\n`en`). 11 Country metric columns and 12 Vendor ranking columns; paired payload keys are\nmerged into `previous→target` cells. Ranking ETA/check cells are delivered strings,\nescaped for HTML only. Ranking tables scroll horizontally; `thead` is sticky.\n\nFormatting helpers: integers without separators; signed nets `+`/`−`/`0`; ratios\n`xx.xx%`; contribution `x.xxxx pp`; nonzero values that round to zero render as\n`<0.01%` / `<0.0001 pp` with direction in words; null relative change with P=0 renders\n零基线上升 / Zero-baseline increase (P=T=0: 无变化 / No change). Missing values render\n未提供 / Not provided, never as zero.\n\n## Query parameters and modes\n\n- `?mode=ops|demo` sets `data-mode` on load (default `ops`); toggle buttons update it\n  and the URL (and drop `t`).\n- `?t=N` (seconds) is the seek/pause harness: pauses the Demo timeline and seeks to `N`.\n  It implies `mode=demo` unless `?mode=` is given. `?mode=demo&t=0` shows the first frame;\n  `?mode=demo&t=99` (>= duration) shows the final state. `body[data-t]` mirrors the time.\n- Harness API: `window.__pastdueTimeline` = `{ duration(), beats(), time(), paused(), play(), pause(), seek(t) }`.\n  The shell uses its own `requestAnimationFrame` timeline (no external library, so the\n  file stays self-contained). If a hand-authored shell uses GSAP instead, the equivalent\n  is `tl.pause(); tl.seek(N)`.\n\n## Ops vs Demo motion\n\n- **Ops (default)**: every section is fully visible immediately. When motion is allowed\n  (no `prefers-reduced-motion`, no `?t=`), ratio cells in `#country-metrics` (`xx.xx%→xx.xx%`)\n  do a short (~0.6 s) count-up and always end on the exact delivered text.\n- **Demo (`data-mode=\"demo\"`)**: one master timeline reveals `[data-beat]` elements in DOM\n  order: `#run` → `#country-metrics` → each `#country-{CODE}` (ranking table) → its\n  `summary` → its `coverage` → `#cross-country` (if present) → `#scope-gaps`. Each beat\n  fades/slides in (opacity + small `translateY`) and ends with its inline style cleared.\n  A play/pause button (`#demo-play`, visible only in Demo) pauses/resumes; after the end\n  it becomes \"Replay\". Autoplays once on entering Demo; never loops.\n- **Demo never omits data.** Only reveal order and opacity change; no `display:none`,\n  no text/number rewriting, and the embedded JSON is untouched. Print always shows\n  everything.\n- Beat markup: `data-beat` on each `<section>`; per-country `summary` / `coverage` blocks are\n  `<div data-beat=\"summary|coverage\">` wrappers around the same `<h3>` + text as Ops.\n\n## Reduced motion\n\n`prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` disables CSS animation/transition, forces `[data-beat]`\nto full opacity, skips the Ops count-up, and makes Demo jump straight to the final state\n(no autoplay, no looping; Play just re-asserts the final state). The JS also listens for\nthe media query changing at runtime.\n\n## Optional isometric hook (not implemented by default)\n\nThe shell contains no isometric/3D visuals. A deployment may opt in by adding its own\ndecorative element (e.g. an inline SVG) inside `#run` marked `data-decor=\"isometric\"` and\ndriving it from `window.__pastdueTimeline.time()`. It must be `aria-hidden`, must not carry\nreport data, and must be disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`.\n## File naming\n\n`pastdue-{mode}-{countries}-{target}.html`, e.g.\n`pastdue-TEST-US-2026-10-23.html`, `pastdue-DAILY-US-CA-2026-10-23.html`.\nLocal/CI samples may use this skill’s `scripts/out/` (`*.html` gitignored for\nskill upload only). Session deliverables use the host working directory\n(e.g. `/sandbox/pastdue-….html`) and must not be deleted after disclose unless\nreplaced by an overwrite in the same turn.\n"
  }
]

HTML companion 已跳过：当前 Markdown 测试报告虽然完成了事实读取，但在本轮输出中没有构建可验证的完整 HTML payload，因此不写入不完整的 HTML 文件。

分析为什么跳过

---

**Cursor**

按这次运行过程看（已加载窄约束后的 skill），结论如下。

### 过程事实

| 阶段 | 证据 |
|---|---|
| 分析 | Atlas `execute_eql` / `analyze_result` 多次 → 事实已读到 |
| HTML skill | 读了 `pastdue-html-report/SKILL.md`、`payload-contract`、`html-shell`（含新闸门/禁空 ranking） |
| 写出 | **没有** `write` 任何 `.html` |
| 删除 | **没有** `rm`（这次不是误删） |
| 结局 | 口头跳过：未构建「可验证完整 HTML payload」 |

所以这次不是「生成后又删掉」，而是 **主动跳过、从未写入**。

### 直接原因

Agent 在读完闸门规则后，判定 **当前拿不出过闸门的 payload**，于是按 skill 选择 **不写文件**（符合「空 ranking / 不完整 payload 不得写」）。

### 更可能的真实原因（结合上一轮行为）

上一轮是：`ranking: []` + Vendor 塞进页面 JS → 现已被硬性禁止。  
这一轮 Agent 没有再造那种残缺 HTML，但也 **没有** 把已读到的 VENDOR_METRICS / ETA / CHECK 填进完整的 `per_country[].ranking[]`（US+CA 各 Top10，工作量大、上下文紧），于是用笼统理由跳过。

注意其措辞是「完成了**事实读取**」，不是「已交付带完整 ranking/ETA 单元格的 Markdown」。若 Markdown 侧 ranking/ETA 本就不齐，跳过 HTML 是对的；若 Markdown 已齐，则是 **有绑定却懒得组 payload**，属于交付偷懒，不是闸门误伤。

### 和「窄约束」的关系

| 点 | 判断 |
|---|---|
| 空 ranking 禁止写 | 生效了（没再写出烂 HTML） |
| 禁止误删 | 无关（根本没写） |
| 副作用 | 过闸门变难后，Agent 更倾向 **跳过** 而不是 **认真组完整 payload** |

### 小结

- **跳过机制**：按新规则，行为合格。  
- **用户要的文件没了**：因为没构建完整 payload，不是 gate 脚本报错，也不是 `rm`。  
- **要改善**：逼 Agent 在 Markdown 已交付时，必须从已有绑定组齐 `ranking[]` 再写；跳过时必须写出 **具体闸门失败项**（例如 `US: empty ranking`），禁止用「未构建 payload」这种模糊话。

若要，我可以再改 skill：跳过须点名失败条件；且当 Markdown ranking 已交付时，禁止以「未构建 payload」作为唯一理由跳过。

---

**User**

这样改有什么影响，是否是一刀切的做法 ，有没有更优雅 高级的方案

---

**Cursor**

你说的「再改」如果是指：

> 跳过须点名失败项；Markdown ranking 已交付时，禁止只用「未构建 payload」跳过  

那确实有影响，也有比「硬禁令」更干净的做法。

### 若按那两条硬写，影响是什么

| 规则 | 好处 | 风险 / 是否一刀切 |
|---|---|---|
| 跳过必须写出具体闸门失败项 | 可审计、可区分真失败 vs 偷懒 | **低风险**，几乎应做 |
| Markdown 已齐则禁止「未构建 payload」跳过 | 逼出完整 HTML | **偏硬**：上下文爆、写文件超时、宿主写失败时，Agent 可能硬写半成品或死循环重试 |

后一条若写成绝对义务，就是一刀切；前一条不是。

### 更优雅的分层方案（推荐）

不要「禁止跳过」，改成 **交付义务 + 合法豁免 + 可观测失败**：

```text
1) 默认义务（Markdown 标准 DAILY/TEST 已交付时）
   → 必须尝试组 payload 并过闸门后写出 HTML

2) 合法跳过（须点名条件，禁止空话）
   - GATE: <具体理由>        （如 US: empty ranking / 缺 selected vendor）
   - CAPACITY: context/output budget 不足以嵌入完整 ranking（须说明 Country/Vendor 规模）
   - HOST: 写文件失败（保留错误原文）
   禁止单独使用：「未构建可验证 payload」

3) 降级（比全跳优雅）
   - 优先：完整 Ops HTML（全 ranking 进 JSON）
   - 若 CAPACITY：仍写 HTML，但按 Country 拆文件
     pastdue-TEST-US-….html + pastdue-TEST-CA-….html
     每文件自洽、各过闸门；聊天里列出全部路径
   - 不推荐：再退回「JS 里塞 Vendor、ranking:[]」（已禁）

4) 观测
   跳过/降级时固定一句：
   HTML: SKIPPED|SPLIT|WRITTEN — reason=GATE|CAPACITY|HOST — detail=…
```

### 为什么比「禁止模糊跳过」高级

- **保留诚实跳过**（真闸门失败、真写失败）  
- **把「偷懒」和「装不下」拆开**：CAPACITY → 拆文件，而不是装成 GATE  
- **不强制 Python**、不恢复空 ranking 烂 HTML  
- **可度量**：以后看日志就知道是 GATE 多还是 CAPACITY 多  

### 建议落地粒度

| 优先级 | 改什么 |
|---|---|
| P0 | 禁止模糊跳过话术；必须 `SKIPPED — reason=… detail=…` |
| P1 | Markdown 已交付时默认必须尝试 HTML；CAPACITY 时允许拆 Country 文件 |
| P2（可选） | `html-shell` 注明 multi-file 命名约定 |

**结论：** 只禁「未构建 payload」一刀切；更优雅的是 **义务尝试 + 具名原因 + CAPACITY 时拆文件降级**。

若要落地，我建议先做 **P0+P1**（skill + 系统提示各一小段），不做绝对「禁止一切跳过」。要我按这个改吗？

### 核心变化
- **默认义务**：Markdown DAILY/TEST 成功后必须尝试组 payload → 过闸门 → 写 HTML  
- **禁止模糊跳过**：「未构建 payload / incomplete payload」不能单独作为理由  
- **具名披露**：`HTML: WRITTEN|SPLIT|SKIPPED — reason=GATE|CAPACITY|HOST|MODE|OK — detail=…`  
- **CAPACITY 降级**：单文件装不下时按 Country 拆文件，每文件自洽过闸门并列出全部路径

[
  {
    "type": "text",
    "text": "# Metrics and time — v3.1 authoritative semantics\r\n\r\n## Time and evidence basis\r\nBusiness timezone: **America/Los_Angeles**, including DST. Completed means\r\nsnapshot_date < today's Los Angeles date, not proof ETL is complete. Run-time\r\nlabels use the instant's actual PST (UTC−08:00) or PDT (UTC−07:00). For example,\r\n2026-10-27 is PDT; 2026-11-03 is PST. Snapshot dates remain YYYY-MM-DD; don't\r\nappend an invented fixed-zone time to a date-only fact.\r\n\r\nWEEKDAYS = Monday–Friday, no holiday exclusions. Default T is latest eligible\r\nactual date; P is nearest earlier eligible actual date independently per Country.\r\nTuesday normally compares Monday to Friday; Monday's run compares Friday to\r\nThursday. PREVIOUS_AVAILABLE uses actual dates, not yesterday arithmetic.\r\nEXPLICIT explore pairs honor both supplied dates or fail; report preset retains\r\nnearest-previous semantics. Range applies to targets; P may precede range start.\r\nCALENDAR_DAYS is an explicit override, not a fallback for missing weekdays.\r\n\r\nExpected target = nearest weekday strictly before D. Lag counts weekdays T+1\r\nthrough expected; >2=STALE, 1..2=DELAYED. Missing between P/T excludes endpoints.\r\nNormal weekends aren't missing workdays. Repeated weekend pairs aren't new events;\r\nwithout persistent host state no claim of cross-run notification deduplication.\r\n\r\nCURRENT is current readable state with capture date, not latest completed snapshot.\r\nSNAPSHOT is a single eligible completed date. PAIR is two actual eligible dates.\r\nRANGE is separate dated snapshot stocks, not a sum of daily stocks. Ignore\r\nnonapplicable previous/change placeholders on non-PAIR results.\r\n\r\n## Country trigger\r\nR_T=PD_T/Open_T; R_P=PD_P/Open_P. Relative=(R_T−R_P)/R_P;\r\npoint-change ratio=R_T−R_P; pp multiplies by 100. Relative ≥0.10 triggers using\r\nunrounded counts/cross-products, no daily normalization of multi-day gaps.\r\nPrevious PD=0 with positive Open in both periods and target PD>0 is\r\nZERO_BASELINE_INCREASE; both PD=0 is normal. Open=0 isn't a valid ratio or zero\r\nbaseline. Negative counts or PD>Open are DATA_ERROR. Missing P is INSUFFICIENT.\r\n\r\n## Shared Vendor report selection\r\nC_T(v)=PD_T(v)/CountryOpen_T; C_P(v)=PD_P(v)/CountryOpen_P;\r\ndelta_C=C_T−C_P. Server compares exact numerator\r\nPD_T(v)*CountryOpen_P−PD_P(v)*CountryOpen_T. Positive numerators descending,\r\nVendor ascending, first top-n≤10; never stop at 80% coverage.\r\nPositive share = positive numerator / sum(all positive numerators).\r\nSelected coverage = selected positive / all positive, NOT fraction of net rise.\r\nAll signed contributions reconcile to Country point change; positive Top-10 need\r\nnot sum to it and can exceed net rise. SINGLE_VENDOR includes zero/negative delta.\r\nVendor ratio=Vendor PD/Vendor Open, not Country Open or past-due-only row count.\r\n\r\n## Flexible metrics, denominators and ranking\r\n`pastdue.explore-fact` uses target/previous-past-due-count and -open-count for all\r\nbases, plus explicitly returned ratio/share denominators. At COUNTRY grain,\r\nratio=group PD/whole Country Open; at Vendor grains ratio=group PD/whole Vendor\r\nOpen, including when grouping by Code/Source. Do NOT equate bucket Open to ratio\r\ndenominator. `denominator-scope` controls SHARE only:\r\n- COUNTRY_ALL: whole Country PD before Vendor/org/ETA filters.\r\n- FILTERED_POPULATION: Vendor+org-filtered PD before ETA filters.\r\n- WITHIN_VENDOR: each scoped Vendor PD before ETA filters; invalid for COUNTRY.\r\nA fully scoped Vendor's WITHIN_VENDOR share can be 100%; this is not its PD rate.\r\n\r\nCurrent Open is current detail-view row count, not proven identical to historical\r\nsnapshot ETL semantics. Org filters use distinct CURRENT_MAPPING, including on\r\nhistorical facts; don't multiply rows or silently discard unmapped Vendors when\r\nno org filter is present. Code trim-uppercase; Source trimmed case-preserving\r\nexact match. Diagnostic AUTO US alias does not rewrite a raw source filter.\r\n\r\nServer ranks grouped FACT rows using PAST_DUE_COUNT (target count), PAST_DUE_RATIO\r\n(target ratio), PAST_DUE_SHARE (target share), COUNT_CHANGE (T−P), RATIO_CHANGE\r\n(target−previous ratio), CONTRIBUTION_CHANGE (delta_C), or NONE (deterministic\r\nidentity order). ASC/DESC is explicit, top-n=0 means all or 1..100 limits rows.\r\nRANGE top-n is across date×grain rows, not per date. Null rank metrics sort last;\r\nties use target date/Vendor/Code/Source. PAIR is required for change ranking or\r\nPOSITIVE/NEGATIVE/ZERO selection. Selection sign follows ratio/contribution change\r\nwhen those are the ranking metric, otherwise count change. ALL includes all signs.\r\n\r\n## ETA shared summaries\r\nFull normalized union of periods contains positive/negative/zero deltas. Empty\r\nCode/Source=UNKNOWN. Missing period bucket may be zero only after valid complete\r\nquery; an error isn't zero. Delta=T−P; relative=delta/P when P>0, otherwise null\r\n(zero-baseline increase or no change); decline to zero is −100%.\r\n\r\nServer ranks abs(delta) DESC, target count DESC, raw normalized value ASC. Report\r\nreturns BUCKET rank≤5 and OTHER rank6; Other sums exact T/P before calculating\r\npercentages. expected-bucket-count counts raw buckets, Other's\r\nother-bucket-count counts represented raw buckets. Never fetch only first five\r\nrecords or recompute remainder from rounded summaries.\r\nIncrease=sum(max(delta,0)); decrease=sum(min(delta,0)); net=increase+decrease.\r\nOTHER's net change alone cannot reconstruct its internal increases/decreases;\r\nuse server signed totals and VENDOR_CHECK. Code and Source each reconcile T/P to\r\nVendor, never add them together. US joint-source reconciles to Code=US, not whole\r\nVendor. Counts exact; unrounded ratios tolerate abs/relative 1e-9. Decimal scale\r\n18 is transport precision, not display precision or exact threshold arithmetic.\r\nNo exposed dataset revision means pinned dates don't prove a transaction snapshot.\r\n\r\n## Status and interpretation\r\nbusiness_status: NORMAL / TRIGGERED / ZERO_BASELINE_INCREASE / NOT_EVALUABLE.\r\ndata_status: OK / DELAYED / STALE / INSUFFICIENT / ACCESS_DENIED / DATA_ERROR.\r\nanalysis_status: COMPLETE / PARTIAL / UNAVAILABLE / NOT_REQUIRED.\r\ndrilldown_reason: THRESHOLD_MET / TEST_FORCED / USER_REQUESTED / NONE.\r\nNormal daily stale/invalid/uncomparable pairs don't enter valid alert drilldown;\r\ntest can inspect valid stale pairs with warnings. Analysis failure does not erase\r\nan independently verified trigger. Current explore doesn't invent a monitor trigger.\r\n\r\nRisk codes are signals, not proven causes. Falling PD with rising contribution\r\ncan reflect shrinking Country Open. Aggregate changes don't prove individual PO\r\nmigration or personal fault. Owner/contacts/PO are CURRENT only. Bilingual rule\r\ntext and explicit AutoUS/AUTO US mapping are in eta-diagnostic-rules.edn.\r\n\r\n## Display precision\r\nRatios/shares/relative changes: TWO decimals %. Contribution and ratio point\r\nchanges: FOUR decimals pp (including paired contributions), after multiplying\r\nratio units by 100. Never use formatted values for thresholds or reconciliation.\r\n\r\n## Rule identity and Source classification\r\neta-diagnostic-rules.edn assigns rule-id, risk-type and requires-joint-facts to\r\nevery bilingual rule. All US interpretations require verified joint Code=US×Source\r\nfacts in any Country. AutoUS/AUTO US match first. OTHER_KNOWN requires exact\r\nmembership in its explicit governed recognized Source set AND presence in an\r\nactual joint row; any unrecognized nonempty raw value is diagnostically UNKNOWN,\r\nnot rough-date. Preserve raw spelling and counts; classification doesn't rewrite\r\nSource identity/filtering or add semantics from a Source name. No new table or\r\nassumed catalog endpoint is required. Mixed OTHER can't establish membership.\r\n\r\n\r\n## Execution versus presentation — Agent 3.3.0\r\n\r\nMetric semantics remain 3.1.0. Server structured summaries/checks, client complete\r\nretrieval, and delivered Vendor analysis are separate scopes. None certifies an\r\nindependent final-output review. The sole layout is report-format.md; diagnostic\r\napplicability is in eta-diagnostic-rules.edn. No Agent-issued overall report PASS.\r\n"
  }
]

  {
    "type": "text",
    "text": "# Retained-result reading — 3.3.16\r\n\r\n## 1. Call boundary\r\n\r\nThis is a call procedure, NOT installed middleware. Prompt/Skill/Atlas EDN cannot\r\nstrip fields from a request after a host has constructed or sent it.\r\n\r\nFor a new source request use the actual discovered `execute_eql` schema:\r\n\r\n```json\r\n{\"query\":\"<EQL with {:entity ... :query ...}>\",\"retain_result\":true}\r\n```\r\n\r\nFor SQL reads of that request's retained result, construct only these keys:\r\n\r\n```json\r\n{\"result_handle\":\"<handle from that execute response>\",\"sql\":\"<SQL for that result>\",\"analysis_version\":2}\r\n```\r\n\r\nThe result selector is exactly one of `query`, `result_handle`, `result_handles`.\r\nUnused selectors are absent, not empty strings, empty arrays or null. Do not send\r\nall three and assume empty values will be ignored. Do not send optional defaults\r\nunnecessarily. A populated alternative selector must never be silently dropped to\r\nmake an ambiguous request pass. `false`/0 are not missing values.\r\n\r\nIf columns are not yet known, an optional schema discovery call is valid when the\r\nlive tool supports it: `{\"result_handle\":\"<actual handle>\",\"analysis_version\":2}`.\r\nIt has succeeded in supplied runs. It retrieves schema/counts, not business rows.\r\nKnown verified column mappings may be reused in the same environment; discovery\r\nis neither mandatory on every read nor the established cause of selector failure.\r\n\r\nFor `give exactly one ...`: inspect the actual submitted object. At most ONE\r\nchanged, evidence-based correction is allowed for the logical request. Remove\r\nunused selectors if the caller can omit them; preserve the real handle. An\r\nidentical resend, version switch or source-query rerun is not a correction.\r\nIf the host still submits conflicting fields or cannot represent the valid shape,\r\nstop dependent reads and report TOOL_ARGUMENT_ERROR with component cause\r\nUNCONFIRMED. Do not claim the Skill executed a cleanup hook or fixed the host.\r\n\r\nKeep actual execute/read call IDs when exposed, connection, input, handle and\r\ncapture/expiry metadata. Read handles only from their originating response, never\r\nfrom an earlier run or a plausible remembered ID. A read response naming another\r\nhandle is ASSOCIATION_ERROR for that result. Missing provenance is UNVERIFIED;\r\nquarantine dependent facts, not independent verified Countries/dimensions. If a\r\nknown current response appears expired, disclose the anomaly; only confirmed\r\nexpiry justifies a fresh execute, not guessing a replacement handle.\r\n\r\n## 2. Small monitor and historical results\r\n\r\nFor the known small monitor result:\r\n\r\n```sql\r\nSELECT * FROM data ORDER BY country_code\r\n```\r\n\r\nRead actual rows before fixing dates/triggers, then apply the requested Country\r\nallowlist. Inspect row AND cell truncation; SELECT * does not guarantee coverage.\r\nHistory results use their discovered explicit columns and supported pagination,\r\nnot a blanket small-table assumption. Do not add report-only fields to monitor.\r\n\r\n## 3. Report typed reads, one captured result\r\n\r\nUse the full report EQL projection in structured-queries.eql before retaining the\r\nresult. Below are disjoint projections of the SAME handle, not new source SQL.\r\nCountry Control is separate from Vendor Metrics to avoid placeholder ambiguity.\r\nThe retained column names below must match the actual environment; if absent,\r\ncheck actual schema, not approximate aliases. An unprojected field is unknown.\r\n\r\n### A. Country Control — read and reconcile first\r\n\r\n```sql\r\nSELECT scope_key, record_type, country_code, vendor_code,\r\n       previous_snapshot_date, target_snapshot_date, calendar_mode,\r\n       selection_mode, metric_version, scope_status, business_timezone, queried_at_local,\r\n       selected_vendor_count, selected_positive_coverage,\r\n       previous_country_past_due_count, target_country_past_due_count,\r\n       previous_country_open, target_country_open,\r\n       previous_country_ratio, target_country_ratio,\r\n       expected_record_count, row_ordinal\r\nFROM data\r\nWHERE record_type = 'COUNTRY_CONTROL'\r\nORDER BY row_ordinal\r\n```\r\n\r\nRequire exactly one control for the requested scope. Match Country/P/T/calendar,\r\nselection, metric version and scope key to the actual request; scope-status must\r\nbe OK. Compare the dedicated Country fields to the pinned monitor/history:\r\n\r\n| Report COUNTRY_CONTROL | Monitor/history |\r\n|---|---|\r\n| previous_country_past_due_count / target_country_past_due_count | previous_past_due_count / target_past_due_count |\r\n| previous_country_open / target_country_open | previous_open_count / target_open_count |\r\n| previous_country_ratio / target_country_ratio | previous_past_due_ratio / target_past_due_ratio |\r\n\r\nCounts must match exactly; apply documented tolerance for unrounded ratios.\r\n`previous_past_due_count`, `target_past_due_count`, `previous_open_count`,\r\n`target_open_count` on COUNTRY_CONTROL are NOT Country totals. Their zeros are\r\nunrelated placeholders, not a failed cross-check. Do not interpret them.\r\n\r\nIf dedicated fields were not read, read them before asserting a mismatch. A true\r\nmismatch is disclosed with both original values/captures; no blanket zero-value\r\nexemption. Preserve independently verified report-scope facts with their dates\r\nand denominators, but do not claim validated attribution of the monitor event\r\nuntil cross-capture reconciliation succeeds. Handle/identity errors are stronger:\r\nno dependent use merely because some numbers look reasonable.\r\n\r\n### B. Vendor Metrics — returned ranking and Vendor quantities only\r\n\r\n```sql\r\nSELECT scope_key, record_type, country_code, vendor_code, vendor_name,\r\n       previous_snapshot_date, target_snapshot_date, calendar_mode,\r\n       selection_mode, metric_version, scope_status,\r\n       contribution_rank, selected_vendor_count,\r\n       previous_past_due_count, target_past_due_count, past_due_count_change,\r\n       previous_open_count, target_open_count, previous_vendor_ratio, target_vendor_ratio,\r\n       previous_country_contribution, target_country_contribution,\r\n       delta_country_contribution, positive_contribution_share, selected_positive_coverage,\r\n       expected_record_count, row_ordinal\r\nFROM data\r\nWHERE record_type = 'VENDOR_METRICS'\r\nORDER BY row_ordinal\r\n```\r\n\r\nUse returned contribution_rank for presentation; do not replace it with read\r\nposition or reselect Vendors. Match the complete set to selected_vendor_count.\r\nZero selected Vendors after OK control is an explicit empty selection, not an\r\nerror or permission to add negative Vendors.\r\n\r\n### C. ETA summaries and checks — complete compact representation\r\n\r\n```sql\r\nSELECT scope_key, record_type, country_code, vendor_code, vendor_name,\r\n       previous_snapshot_date, target_snapshot_date, calendar_mode,\r\n       selection_mode, metric_version, scope_status,\r\n       dimension, eta_value, row_kind, display_rank, other_bucket_count,\r\n       previous_past_due_count, target_past_due_count, past_due_count_change,\r\n       past_due_count_relative_change, delta_country_contribution,\r\n       expected_bucket_count, increase_total, decrease_total, net_change,\r\n       previous_total, target_total, previous_expected_total, target_expected_total,\r\n       counts_match, required, expected_record_count, row_ordinal\r\nFROM data\r\nWHERE record_type IN ('ETA_CODE_SUMMARY','ETA_SOURCE_SUMMARY','US_SOURCE_SUMMARY','VENDOR_CHECK')\r\nORDER BY row_ordinal\r\n```\r\n\r\nSummary count fields refer to that bucket/Other, NOT Vendor totals. Read the\r\nsigned dimension totals from VENDOR_CHECK: previous_total, target_total,\r\nincrease_total, decrease_total, net_change and the expected totals. `us` expected\r\ntotals are conditional Code=US, not whole Vendor PD. Unrelated sparse fields on\r\ncheck records are not substitute check values. Keep Code and Source separate.\r\n\r\n### Paging and completion\r\n\r\nThe three projections together must cover the retained source result, including\r\ncontrol and checks. Keep a compact per-Country ledger: originating call/handle,\r\nscope/P/T/version, expected source count, actual unique ordinals, selected Vendor\r\ncodes/ranks, and each dimension's retrieval/check/delivery state. This is an Agent\r\nprocedure, not a new persisted service or a substitute original evidence.\r\n\r\nWhen a read is truncated or too large, partition C losslessly by known record_type\r\nor stable row_ordinal windows using the live reader's supported WHERE/ORDER/LIMIT\r\nsyntax. Keep every identity column in every partition. Continue from the last\r\nactually read ordinal; don't assume an omitted row was zero. Do not drop summaries\r\nor checks to reduce output, and do not compare one partition's size to the whole\r\nsource count. Unexpected record types must be read/disclosed, not silently filtered.\r\nInspect row and cell flags on EVERY page. `row-count` is the retained table size.\r\n`truncation.rows-total` and `truncation.rows-shown` are this SQL's output.\r\nThis SQL is complete when `rows?` is false, `cells?` is false, and `rows-shown`\r\nequals `rows-total`. Render those rows in the report modules.\r\n\r\n`expected_record_count` is the whole compact report, not one WHERE clause.\r\nAdd `rows-total` across the disjoint statements. When that sum equals\r\n`expected_record_count`, retrieval is complete. Ten Vendor Metrics rows plus\r\n167 other rows is 177: the other statement kept the Vendor rows, it did not\r\ndrop them. Same for 10 plus 144 against 154. Deliver Code, Source, signed\r\ntotals and the diagnosis from those returned rows.\r\n\r\nTreat a read as truncated only when `rows?` is true, `cells?` is true, or\r\n`rows-shown` is below `rows-total`. Then continue from the last `row_ordinal`.\r\nDo not withhold the ETA section while its rows are already in a complete response.\r\nThe union of unique ordinals must still equal `expected_record_count`.\r\nThis verifies transport of the compact result, not all raw ETA buckets.\r\n\r\nSource collection pagination is separate: a handle can contain only one source\r\npage. Follow the playbook's source coverage rules. Never requery source per Vendor\r\nto fix retained preview truncation. When a full read path is unavailable, name the\r\nprecise gap and keep dependent scope unverified rather than claim completion.\r\n"
